\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\Rightarrow}

\displaystyle([d^{t}]^{-(r+1)})^{\tfrac{r}{r+1}}>\kappa^{\tfrac{r}{r+1}}\cdot([d^{t-1}]^{-(r+1)})^{\tfrac{r}{r+1}}

\displaystyle\overset{}{\Rightarrow}

\displaystyle[d^{t}]^{-r}>\kappa^{\tfrac{r}{r+1}}\cdot[d^{t-1}]^{-r},

where step
①
uses the definition of
g(\cdot)
; step
②
uses the fact that if
a>b>0
, then
a^{\dot{r}}>b^{\dot{r}}
for any exponent
\dot{r}\triangleq\tfrac{r}{r+1}\in(0,1)
. We further derive:

\displaystyle\textstyle[d^{t}]^{-r}-[d^{t-1}]^{-r}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\geq}

\displaystyle\textstyle(\kappa^{\tfrac{r}{r+1}}-1)\cdot[d^{t-1}]^{-r}

(18)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\geq}

\displaystyle\textstyle(\kappa^{\tfrac{r}{r+1}}-1)\cdot[d^{0}]^{-r},

where step
①
uses Inequality (
17
); step
②
uses
r>0
and
d^{t-1}\leq d^{0}
for all
t
.

In view of Inequalities (
16
) and (
18
), we have:

\displaystyle[d^{t}]^{-r}-[d^{t-1}]^{-r}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\textstyle\min(\tfrac{\kappa^{-1}r}{c^{1/u}t^{p}},(\kappa^{\tfrac{r}{r+1}}-1)\cdot[d^{0}]^{-r})

(19)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathcal{O}(\tfrac{1}{t^{p}}).

We now focus on Inequality (
19
). Telescoping Inequality (
19
) over
t=\{1,2,\ldots,T\}
, we have:

\displaystyle\textstyle[d^{T}]^{-r}-[d^{0}]^{-r}\geq\textstyle\mathcal{O}(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tfrac{1}{t^{p}})\overset{\text{①}}{=}\mathcal{O}((1-p)T^{1-p})=\mathcal{O}(T^{1-p}),

where step
①
use Lemma
A.5
. This leads to:

\displaystyle d^{T}=([d^{T}]^{-r})^{-1/r}\leq\mathcal{O}(T^{1-p})^{-1/r}=\mathcal{O}(T^{-\zeta}).

∎

Lemma A.17
.

Assume that
d^{t}/d^{t-1}\leq\tfrac{ct^{q}}{ct^{q}+1}
, where
c\geq 0
and
q\in(0,1)
. Then we have:
d^{t}\leq\mathcal{O}({1}/{\exp(t^{\zeta})})
, where
\zeta=1-q
.

Proof.

We define
\gamma^{t}\triangleq\tfrac{1}{ct^{q}+1}\in(0,1)
.

First, we derive the following results:

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\gamma^{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tfrac{1}{ct^{q}+1}

(20)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\geq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{1+c}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tfrac{1}{t^{q}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\geq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{1+c}(1-q)T^{(1-q)}=\mathcal{O}(T^{1-q}),

where step
①
uses
ct^{q}+1\leq(1+c)t^{q}
since
t^{q}\geq 1
if
t\geq 1
; step
②
uses Lemma
A.5
that
(1-p)T^{(1-p)}\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tfrac{1}{t^{p}}
for all
p\in(0,1)
.

Second, noticing that
\tfrac{d^{t}}{d^{t-1}}=\tfrac{ct^{q}}{ct^{q}+1}=1-\gamma^{t}
, we have:

\displaystyle\tfrac{d^{T}}{d^{0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\gamma^{1})(1-\gamma^{2})(1-\gamma^{3})\ldots(1-\gamma^{T}).

(21)

This further leads to:

\displaystyle\textstyle d^{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\textstyle\exp(\log(d^{T}))

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\exp(\log(d^{0}\cdot\prod_{t=1}^{T}(1-\gamma^{t})))

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\exp(\log(d^{0})+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log(1-\gamma^{t}))

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\exp(\log(d^{0})+\sum_{t=1}^{T}(-\gamma^{t}))

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\exp(\log(d^{0}))\times\{\exp(\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\gamma^{t}))\}^{-1}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑤}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle d^{0}\times\{\exp(\mathcal{O}(T^{(1-q)}))\}^{-1}=\mathcal{O}({1}/{\exp(T^{1-q})}),

where step
①
uses Inequality (
21
); step
②
uses
\log(ab)=\log(a)+\log(b)
for all
a,b>0
; step
③
uses
\log(1-x)\leq-x
for all
x\in(0,1)
, and
1-\gamma^{t}\in(0,1)
for all
t
; step
④
uses
\exp(a+b)=\exp(a)\exp(b)
for all
a,b>0
; step
⑤
uses Inequality (
20
).

∎

Appendix B
Proofs for Section
2

B.1
Proof of Lemma
2.3

Proof.

Assume
0<\mu_{2}<\mu_{1}<\frac{1}{W_{h}}
, and fixing
\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
.

We define
h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\triangleq\min_{\mathbf{v}}h(\mathbf{v})+\frac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{v}-\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}
, and
\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})=\textstyle\arg\min_{\mathbf{v}}\,h(\mathbf{v})+\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{v}-\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}
.

We define
h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})\triangleq\min_{\mathbf{v}}h(\mathbf{v})+\frac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{v}-\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}
, and
\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})=\textstyle\arg\min_{\mathbf{v}}\,h(\mathbf{v})+\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{v}-\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}
.

By the optimality of
\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})
and
\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle\in

\displaystyle\textstyle\mu_{1}\partial h(\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})),

(22)

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle\in

\displaystyle\textstyle\mu_{2}\partial h(\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})).

(23)

Part (a)
. We now prove that
0\leq h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})-h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})
. For any
\mathbf{s}_{1}\in\partial h(\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y}))
and
\mathbf{s}_{2}\in\partial h(\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y}))
, we have:

\displaystyle h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})-h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}+h(\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y}))-h(\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y}))

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})-\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y}),\mathbf{s}_{1}\rangle+\tfrac{W_{h}}{2}\|\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{1}\rangle+\tfrac{W_{h}}{2}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{1}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{1}\rangle+\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{1}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot(1-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\mu_{1}})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑤}}{\leq}

\displaystyle 0,

where step
①
uses the definition of
h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})
and
h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})
; step
②
uses weakly convexity of
h(\cdot)
; step
③
uses the optimality of
\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})
and
\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})
in Equations (
24
) and (
25
); step
④
uses
W_{h}\leq\tfrac{1}{\mu_{1}}
; step
⑤
uses
1\geq\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\mu_{1}}
.

Part (b)
. We now prove that
h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})-h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\leq\min\{\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2\mu_{2}},1\}\cdot(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2})C_{h}^{2}
. For any
\mathbf{s}_{1}\in\partial h(\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y}))
and
\mathbf{s}_{2}\in\partial h(\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y}))
, we have:

\displaystyle h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})-h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}+h(\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y}))-h(\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y}))

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y}),\mathbf{s}_{1}\rangle+\tfrac{W_{h}}{2}\|\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{1}\rangle+\tfrac{W_{h}}{2}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}\langle\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}\rangle+\tfrac{W_{h}}{2}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\min\{-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}\langle\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}\rangle+\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}\langle\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}\rangle+\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\min\{(-\mu_{2}+\mu_{1})\cdot\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2},(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2})\langle\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}\rangle-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\tfrac{\mu_{2}^{2}}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑤}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\min\{\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2}),(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2})\langle\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}\rangle\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑥}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\min\{\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2\mu_{2}}\cdot(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2}),(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2})\}\cdot C_{h}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\min\{\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2\mu_{2}},1\}\cdot(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2})\cdot C_{h}^{2},

where step
①
uses the definition of
h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})
and
h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})
; step
②
uses the weakly convexity of
h(\cdot)
; step
③
uses the optimality of
\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})
and
\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})
in Equations (
24
) and (
25
); step
④
uses
W_{h}\leq\frac{1}{\mu_{1}}
and
W_{h}\leq\frac{1}{\mu_{2}}
; step
⑤
uses
\mu_{2}\leq\mu_{1}
; step
⑥
uses
\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|\leq C_{h}
,
\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|\leq C_{h}
, and
\langle\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}\rangle\leq\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|\leq C_{h}^{2}
.

∎

B.2
Proof of Lemma
2.3

Proof.

Assume
0<\mu_{2}<\mu_{1}<\frac{1}{W_{h}}
, and fixing
\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
.

We define
h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\triangleq\min_{\mathbf{v}}h(\mathbf{v})+\frac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{v}-\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}
, and
\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})=\textstyle\arg\min_{\mathbf{v}}\,h(\mathbf{v})+\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{v}-\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}
.

We define
h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})\triangleq\min_{\mathbf{v}}h(\mathbf{v})+\frac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{v}-\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}
, and
\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})=\textstyle\arg\min_{\mathbf{v}}\,h(\mathbf{v})+\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{v}-\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}
.

By the optimality of
\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})
and
\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle\in

\displaystyle\textstyle\mu_{1}\partial h(\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y}))

(24)

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle\in

\displaystyle\textstyle\mu_{2}\partial h(\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})).

(25)

Part (a)
. We now prove that
0\leq h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})-h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})
. For any
\mathbf{s}_{1}\in\partial h(\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y}))
and
\mathbf{s}_{2}\in\partial h(\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y}))
, we have:

\displaystyle h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})-h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}+h(\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y}))-h(\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y}))

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})-\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y}),\mathbf{s}_{1}\rangle+\tfrac{W_{h}}{2}\|\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{1}\rangle+\tfrac{W_{h}}{2}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{1}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{1}\rangle+\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{1}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot(1-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\mu_{1}})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑤}}{\leq}

\displaystyle 0,

where step
①
uses the definition of
h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})
and
h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})
; step
②
uses weakly convexity of
h(\cdot)
; step
③
uses the optimality of
\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})
and
\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})
in Equations (
24
) and (
25
); step
④
uses
W_{h}\leq\tfrac{1}{\mu_{1}}
; step
⑤
uses
1\geq\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\mu_{1}}
.

Part (b)
. We now prove that
h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})-h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\leq\min\{\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2\mu_{2}},1\}\cdot(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2})C_{h}^{2}
. For any
\mathbf{s}_{1}\in\partial h(\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y}))
and
\mathbf{s}_{2}\in\partial h(\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y}))
, we have:

\displaystyle h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})-h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}+h(\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y}))-h(\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y}))

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y}),\mathbf{s}_{1}\rangle+\tfrac{W_{h}}{2}\|\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})-\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{1}\rangle+\tfrac{W_{h}}{2}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}\langle\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}\rangle+\tfrac{W_{h}}{2}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\min\{-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}\langle\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}\rangle+\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}\langle\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}\rangle+\tfrac{1}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mu_{1}\mathbf{s}_{2}-\mu_{2}\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\min\{(-\mu_{2}+\mu_{1})\cdot\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2},(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2})\langle\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}\rangle-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\tfrac{\mu_{2}^{2}}{2\mu_{1}}\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑤}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\min\{\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2\mu_{2}}\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2}),(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2})\langle\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}\rangle\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑥}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\min\{\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2\mu_{2}}\cdot(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2}),(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2})\}\cdot C_{h}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\min\{\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{2\mu_{2}},1\}\cdot(\mu_{1}-\mu_{2})\cdot C_{h}^{2},

where step
①
uses the definition of
h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})
and
h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})
; step
②
uses the weakly convexity of
h(\cdot)
; step
③
uses the optimality of
\prox_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})
and
\prox_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})
in Equations (
24
) and (
25
); step
④
uses
W_{h}\leq\frac{1}{\mu_{1}}
and
W_{h}\leq\frac{1}{\mu_{2}}
; step
⑤
uses
\mu_{2}\leq\mu_{1}
; step
⑥
uses
\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|\leq C_{h}
,
\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|\leq C_{h}
, and
\langle\mathbf{s}_{1},\mathbf{s}_{2}\rangle\leq\|\mathbf{s}_{1}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{s}_{2}\|\leq C_{h}^{2}
.

∎

B.3
Proof of Lemma
2.4

Proof.

Assume
0<\mu_{2}<\mu_{1}\leq\tfrac{1}{2W_{h}}
, and fixing
\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
.

Using the result in Lemma
2.2
, we establish that the gradient of
h_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})

w.r.t

\mathbf{y}
can be computed as:

\displaystyle\nabla h_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})=\mu^{-1}(\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})).

The gradient of the mapping
\nabla h_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})

w.r.t.
the variable
1/\mu
can be computed as:
\nabla_{1/\mu}\left(\nabla h_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})\right)=\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})
. We further obtain:

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{1/\mu}\left(\nabla h_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})\right)\|=\|\mathbf{y}-\prox_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})\|\overset{\text{①}}{=}\mu\|\partial h(\prox_{\mu}(\mathbf{y}))\|\leq\mu C_{h}.

Here, step
①
uses the optimality of
\prox_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})
that:
\mathbf{0}\in\partial h(\prox_{\mu}(\mathbf{y}))+\tfrac{1}{\mu}(\prox_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})-\mathbf{y})
. Therefore, for all
\mu\in(0,\tfrac{1}{2W_{h}}]
, we have:

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla h_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})-\nabla h_{\mu^{\prime}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}}{|1/\mu-1/\mu^{\prime}|}\leq\mu C_{h}.

Letting
\mu=\mu_{1}
and
\mu^{\prime}=\mu_{2}
, we have:
\|\nabla h_{\mu_{1}}(\mathbf{y})-\nabla h_{\mu_{2}}(\mathbf{y})\|_{2}\leq|1-\mu_{1}/\mu_{2}|C_{h}=(\mu_{1}/\mu_{2}-1)C_{h}
.

∎

B.4
Proof of Lemma
2.5

Proof.

We consider the following optimization problem:

\displaystyle\bar{\mathbf{y}}=\arg\min_{\mathbf{y}}h_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})+\tfrac{\beta}{2}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{b}\|_{2}^{2}.

(26)

Given
h_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})
being
(\mu^{-1})
-weakly convex and
\beta>\mu^{-1}
, Problem (
26
) becomes strongly convex and has a unique optimal solution, which leads to the following equivalent problem:

\displaystyle\textstyle(\bar{\mathbf{y}},\breve{\mathbf{y}})=\arg\min_{\mathbf{y},\mathbf{y}^{\prime}}h(\mathbf{y}^{\prime})+\tfrac{1}{2\mu}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\tfrac{\beta}{2}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{b}\|_{2}^{2},

We have the following first-order optimality conditions for
(\bar{\mathbf{y}},\breve{\mathbf{y}})
:

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\mu}(\bar{\mathbf{y}}-\breve{\mathbf{y}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta(\mathbf{b}-\bar{\mathbf{y}})

(27)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\mu}(\bar{\mathbf{y}}-\breve{\mathbf{y}})

\displaystyle\in

\displaystyle\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}}).

(28)

Part (a)
. We have the following results:

\displaystyle\mathbf{0}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\in}

\displaystyle\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}})+\tfrac{1}{\mu}(\breve{\mathbf{y}}-\bar{\mathbf{y}})

(29)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}})+\tfrac{1}{\mu}(\breve{\mathbf{y}}-\tfrac{1}{1/\mu+\beta}(\tfrac{1}{\mu}\breve{\mathbf{y}}+\beta\mathbf{b}))

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}})+\tfrac{\beta}{1+\mu\beta}(\breve{\mathbf{y}}-\mathbf{b}),

where step
①
uses Equality (
28
); step
②
uses Equality (
27
) that
\bar{\mathbf{y}}=\tfrac{1}{1/\mu+\beta}(\tfrac{1}{\mu}\breve{\mathbf{y}}+\beta\mathbf{b})
. The inclusion in (
29
) implies that:

\displaystyle\breve{\mathbf{y}}=\arg\min_{\mathbf{v}}h(\breve{\mathbf{y}})+\tfrac{1}{2}\cdot\tfrac{\beta}{1+\mu\beta}\|\breve{\mathbf{y}}-\mathbf{b}\|_{2}^{2}.

Part (b)
. Combining Equalities (
27
) and (
28
), we have:
\beta(\mathbf{b}-\bar{\mathbf{y}})\in\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}})
.

Part (c)
. In view of Equation (
28
), we have:
\bar{\mathbf{y}}-\breve{\mathbf{y}}=\mu\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}})
, leading to:
\|\breve{\mathbf{y}}-\bar{\mathbf{y}}\|\leq\mu C_{h}
.

∎

B.5
Proofs for Lemma
2.11

Proof.

We let
\bm{\Delta}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}
and
\mathbf{X}\in\mathcal{M}
. We define
\mathbf{U}\triangleq\bm{\Delta}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
.

We derive the following results:

\displaystyle\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{M}}(\bm{\Delta})\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\|\bm{\Delta}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\|\bm{\Delta}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}(\bm{\Delta}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}+\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\bm{\Delta})\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\|\bm{\Delta}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{4}\|\mathbf{X}(\bm{\Delta}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}+\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\bm{\Delta})\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\langle\bm{\Delta},\mathbf{X}(\bm{\Delta}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}+\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\bm{\Delta})\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{4}\|\bm{\Delta}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}+\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\bm{\Delta}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\langle\bm{\Delta},\mathbf{X}(\bm{\Delta}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}+\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\bm{\Delta})\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{4}\|\mathbf{U}+\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\langle\mathbf{U}+\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}},\mathbf{U}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{4}\|\mathbf{U}+\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\langle\mathbf{U}+\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}},\mathbf{U}+\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\rangle\cdot\tfrac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{4}\|\mathbf{U}+\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\leq 0,

where step
①
uses
\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{M}}(\bm{\Delta})=\bm{\Delta}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}(\bm{\Delta}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}+\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\bm{\Delta})
for all
\bm{\Delta}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}

Absil et al. (2008a)
; step
②
uses the fact that
\|\mathbf{X}\mathbf{P}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}=\tr(\mathbf{P}\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{P}^{\mathsf{T}})=\|\mathbf{P}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}
for all
\mathbf{X}\in\mathcal{M}
; step
③
uses the definition of
\mathbf{U}\triangleq\bm{\Delta}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}
; step
④
uses the symmetric properties of the matrix
(\mathbf{U}+\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})
.

∎

B.6
Proof of Lemma
2.12

Proof.

We let
\rho>0
,
\mathbf{G}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}
, and
\mathbf{X}\in\mathcal{M}
.

We define
\mathbf{U}\triangleq\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}
, and
\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\triangleq\mathbf{G}-\rho\mathbf{X}\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}-(1-\rho)\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{G}
.

First, we have the following equalities:

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{G},\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{G},\mathbf{G}-\rho\mathbf{X}\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}-(1-\rho)\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{G}\rangle

(30)

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{G},\mathbf{G}\rangle-\rho\tr(\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X})-(1-\rho)\tr(\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{G})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{G},\mathbf{G}\rangle-\rho\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U})-(1-\rho)\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}),

where step
①
uses
\mathbf{U}\triangleq\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}
.

Second, we derive the following equalities:

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\rho\mathbf{X}\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}+(1-\rho)\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{G},\rho\mathbf{X}\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}+(1-\rho)\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{G}-\mathbf{G}\rangle

(31)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\rho^{2}\tr(\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{U})+\rho(1-\rho)\tr(\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})-\rho\tr(\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})

\displaystyle+(1-\rho)\rho\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U})+(1-\rho)^{2}\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})-(1-\rho)\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})

\displaystyle-\rho\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U})-(1-\rho)\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})+\langle\mathbf{G},\mathbf{G}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle(2\rho^{2}-1)\cdot\tr(\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{U})-2\rho^{2}\cdot\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U})+\langle\mathbf{G},\mathbf{G}\rangle,

where step
①
uses
\mathbf{U}\triangleq\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}
and
\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
; step
②
uses
\tr(\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})=\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U})
.

Third, we have:

\displaystyle\tr(\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{G})-\tr(\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{U})\overset{\text{①}}{=}\langle\mathbf{G}\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}},\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\rangle\overset{\text{②}}{\geq}0,

(32)

where step
①
uses
\mathbf{U}\triangleq\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}
; step
②
uses the fact that the matrix
(\mathbf{I}_{n}-\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}})
only contains eigenvalues that are
0
or
1
.

Part (a-i)
. We now prove that
\max(1,2\rho)\langle\mathbf{G},\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\rangle\geq\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}
. We discuss two cases. Case (
i
):
\rho\in(0,\frac{1}{2}]
. We have:

\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\langle\mathbf{G},\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\rangle\overset{\text{①}}{=}(2\rho^{2}-\rho)\cdot(\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})-\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}))\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}0,

where step
①
uses Inequalities (
30
) and (
31
); step
②
uses
2\rho^{2}-\rho\leq 0
for all
\rho\in(0,\frac{1}{2}]
, and
\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U})\leq\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})
for all
\mathbf{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
.

Case (
ii
):
\rho\in[\frac{1}{2},\infty)
. We have:

\textstyle\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-2\rho\langle\mathbf{G},\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\rangle\overset{\text{①}}{=}(2\rho-1)(\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})-\langle\mathbf{G},\mathbf{G}\rangle)\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}0,

where step
①
uses Inequalities (
30
) and (
31
); step
②
uses
2\rho-1\geq 0
for all
\rho\in[\frac{1}{2},\infty)
, and Inequality(
32
). Therefore, we conclude that:
\max(1,2\rho)\langle\mathbf{G},\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\rangle\geq\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}
.

Part (a-ii)
. We now prove that
\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\geq\min(1,\rho^{2})\|\mathbb{G}_{1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}
. We consider two cases. Case (
i
):
\rho\in(0,1]
. We have:

\textstyle\rho^{2}\|\mathbb{G}_{1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}}\overset{\text{①}}{=}\textstyle(1-\rho^{2})(\tr(\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{U})-\langle\mathbf{G},\mathbf{G}\rangle)\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}0,

where step
①
uses Inequalities (
30
) and (
31
); step
②
uses
1-\rho^{2}\geq 0
, and Inequality (
32
).

Case (
ii
):
\rho\in(1,\infty)
. We have:

\|\mathbb{G}_{1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}}\overset{\text{①}}{=}(2-2\rho^{2})(\tr(\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{U})-\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}))\overset{}{\leq}0,

where step
①
uses Inequality (
31
); step
②
uses
4\rho^{2}-1\leq 0
for all
\rho\in(0,\frac{1}{2}]
, and the fact that
\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U})-\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})\leq 0
for all
\mathbf{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
. Therefore, we conclude that:
\min(1,\rho^{2})\|\mathbb{G}_{1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\leq\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}
.

Part (b-i)
. We now prove that
\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\geq\min(1,2\rho)\|\mathbb{G}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
. We consider two cases. Case (
i
):
\rho\in(0,\frac{1}{2}]
. We have:

(2\rho)^{2}\|\mathbb{G}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\overset{\text{①}}{=}(4\rho^{2}-1)\cdot(\tr(\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{G})-\tr(\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{U}))\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}0,

where step
①
uses Inequality (
31
); step
②
uses
4\rho^{2}-1\leq 0
for all
\rho\in(0,\frac{1}{2}]
, and Inequality (
32
).

Case (
ii
):
\rho\in(\frac{1}{2},\infty)
. We have:

\|\mathbb{G}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\overset{\text{①}}{=}(2\rho^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2})\cdot(\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U})-\tr(\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{U}))\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}0,

where step
①
uses Inequalities (
30
) and (
31
); step
②
uses
2\rho^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2}\geq 0
for all
\rho\in(\frac{1}{2},\infty)
, and the fact that
\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U})-\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})\leq 0
for all
\mathbf{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
. Therefore, we conclude that
\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\geq\min(1,2\rho)\|\mathbb{G}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
.

Part (b-ii)
. We now prove that
\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\max(1,2\rho)\|\mathbb{G}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
. We consider two cases. Case (
i
):
\rho\in(0,\frac{1}{2}]
. We have:

\|\mathbb{G}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\overset{\text{①}}{=}(2\rho^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2})\cdot(\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U})-\tr(\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{U}))\overset{\text{②}}{\geq}0,

where step
①
uses Inequality (
31
); step
②
uses
2\rho^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2}\leq 0
for all
\rho\in(0,\frac{1}{2}]
, and the fact that
\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U})-\tr(\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}})\leq 0
for all
\mathbf{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
.

Case (
ii
):
\rho\in(\frac{1}{2},\infty)
. We have:

(2\rho)^{2}\|\mathbb{G}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\overset{\text{①}}{=}(4\rho^{2}-1)\cdot(\tr(\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{G})-\tr(\mathbf{U}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{U}))\overset{\text{②}}{\geq}0,

where step
①
uses Inequalities (
30
) and (
31
); step
②
uses
4\rho^{2}-1\geq 0
for all
\rho\in(\frac{1}{2},\infty)
, and Inequality (
32
). Therefore, we conclude that:
\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\geq\min(1,2\rho)\|\mathbb{G}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
.

∎

B.7
Proof of Lemma
2.13

Proof.

Recall that the following first-order optimality conditions are equivalent for all
\mathbf{X}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}
:

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{0}\in\partial\iota_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{X})+\nabla f(\mathbf{X})\right)\Leftrightarrow\left(\mathbf{0}\in\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{M}}(\nabla f(\mathbf{X}))\right).

(33)

Therefore, we derive the following results:

\displaystyle\dist(\mathbf{0},\partial\iota_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{X})+\nabla f(\mathbf{X}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\inf_{\mathbf{R}\in\nabla f(\mathbf{X})+\partial\iota_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{X})}\|\mathbf{R}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\inf_{\mathbf{R}\in\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{M}}(\nabla f(\mathbf{X}))}\|\mathbf{R}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{M}}(\nabla f(\mathbf{X}))\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle\|\nabla f(\mathbf{X})-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}(\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\nabla f(\mathbf{X})+\nabla f(\mathbf{X})^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{I}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}})(\nabla f(\mathbf{X})-\mathbf{X}\nabla f(\mathbf{X})^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\|\nabla f(\mathbf{X})-\mathbf{X}\nabla f(\mathbf{X})^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}\|_{\mathsf{F}},

where step
①
uses Formulation (
33
); step
②
uses
\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{M}}(\bm{\Delta})=\bm{\Delta}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}(\bm{\Delta}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}+\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\bm{\Delta})
for all
\bm{\Delta}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}

Absil et al. (2008a)
; step
③
uses the norm inequality
\|\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\|\mathbf{A}\|\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
, and fact that the matrix
\mathbf{I}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}
only contains eigenvalues that are
\frac{1}{2}
or
1
.

∎

Appendix C
Proofs for Section
4

C.1
Proof of Lemma
4.1

Proof.

We define
\mu^{t}=\tau/\beta^{t}
.

We define
L(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z},\beta)\triangleq f(\mathbf{X})-g(\mathbf{X})+h_{\tau/\beta}(\mathbf{y})+\langle\mathbf{z},\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})-\mathbf{y}\rangle+\frac{\beta}{2}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})-\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}
.

Part (a-i)
. Using the first-order optimality condition of
\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\in\arg\min_{\mathbf{y}}L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})
in Algorithm
1
, for all
t\geq 0
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbf{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+\beta^{t}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t})+\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})

(34)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+\beta^{t}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t}+\beta^{t}(\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\mathbf{z}^{t}+\beta^{t}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1}))

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\mathbf{z}^{t}+\tfrac{1}{\sigma}(\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t+1}),

where step
①
uses
\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})=-\mathbf{z}^{t}+\beta^{t}(\mathbf{y}-\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1}))
; step
②
uses
\mathbf{z}^{t+1}=\mathbf{z}^{t}+\sigma\beta^{t}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1})
.

Part (a-ii)
. We obtain:

\displaystyle\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t+1})-\mathbf{z}^{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\ni}

\displaystyle\beta^{t}(\mathbf{b}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\mathbf{z}^{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle\beta^{t}\mathbf{y}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\mathcal{S}^{t}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-\beta^{t}\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle\beta^{t}\mathbf{y}^{t}-\beta^{t}(\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1}))-\beta^{t}\mathbf{y}^{t+1}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\beta^{t}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\sigma}(\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}),

where step
①
uses the result in Lemma
2.5
that
\beta^{t}(\mathbf{b}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\in\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t+1})
; step
②
uses
\mathbf{b}\triangleq\mathbf{y}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\mathcal{S}^{t}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})/\beta^{t}
, as shown in Algorithm
1
; step
③
uses
\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\mathcal{S}^{t}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})=-\mathbf{z}^{t}+\beta^{t}(\mathbf{y}-\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1}))
; step
④
uses
\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}=\sigma\beta^{t}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1})
.

Part (b)
. First, we derive:

\displaystyle\|\nabla h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\|

(35)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\|\nabla h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})\|+\|\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\|\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})\|+\tfrac{1}{\mu^{t}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle C_{h}(\tfrac{\mu^{t-1}}{\mu^{t}}-1)+\tfrac{1}{\mu^{t}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{=}

\displaystyle C_{h}(\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{\beta^{t-1}}-1)+\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{\tau}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|,

where step
①
uses
\|\mathbf{a}-\mathbf{b}\|\leq\|\mathbf{a}-\mathbf{c}\|+\|\mathbf{c}-\mathbf{b}\|
; step
②
uses the fact that the function
h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y})
is
\tfrac{1}{\mu^{t}}
-smooth
w.r.t.

\mathbf{y}
that:
\|\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})\|\leq\tfrac{1}{\mu^{t}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|
; step
③
uses the fact that
\|\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})\|\leq({\mu^{t-1}}/{\mu^{t}}-1)C_{h}
which holds due to Lemma
2.4
; step
④
uses
\mu^{t}=\tfrac{\tau}{\beta^{t}}
.

Second, we have from Equality (
34
):

\displaystyle\forall t\geq 0,~\mathbf{0}\in\sigma\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\sigma\mathbf{z}^{t}+(\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t+1}),

\displaystyle\forall t\geq 1,~\mathbf{0}\in\sigma\nabla h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\sigma\mathbf{z}^{t-1}+(\mathbf{z}^{t-1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}).

Combining these two equalities yields:

\displaystyle\forall t\geq 1,\,\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}=(\sigma-1)(\mathbf{z}^{t-1}-\mathbf{z}^{t})+\sigma(\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\nabla h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t}).

Applying Lemma
A.2
with
\mathbf{a}^{+}=\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}
,
\mathbf{a}=\mathbf{z}^{t-1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}
,
\mathbf{b}=\sigma\{\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\nabla h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})\}
, and
\varrho=\sigma-1\in[0,1)
, we have:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{\varrho}{1-\varrho}(\|\mathbf{z}^{t-1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{\sigma^{2}}{(1-\varrho)^{2}}\|\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\nabla h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\tfrac{2\sigma^{2}}{(1-\varrho)^{2}}\cdot\tfrac{(\beta^{t})^{2}}{\tau^{2}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\tfrac{2\sigma^{2}}{(1-\varrho)^{2}}\cdot C^{2}_{h}(\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{\beta^{t-1}}-1)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\underbrace{\tfrac{2\sigma^{2}}{(1-\varrho)^{2}}\tfrac{1}{\tau^{2}}}_{\triangleq\dot{\sigma}}\cdot(\beta^{t})^{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\underbrace{\tfrac{2\sigma^{2}}{(1-\varrho)^{2}}\cdot C^{2}_{h}\cdot\tfrac{6}{p}}_{\triangleq\ddot{\sigma}}\cdot(\tfrac{\beta^{0}}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{\beta^{0}}{\beta^{t+1}}),

where step
①
uses Inequality (
35
), and the inequality
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
for all
a,b\in\mathbb{R}
; step
②
uses Lemma
A.9
that
(\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{\beta^{t-1}}-1)^{2}\leq\frac{6\beta^{0}}{p}(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})
for all
t\geq 1
;

∎

C.2
Proof of Lemma
4.3

Proof.

Part (a)
. We have:

\displaystyle\beta^{t+1}-\beta^{t}\cdot(1+\xi)\overset{\text{①}}{=}\beta^{0}\xi(t+1)^{p}-\beta^{0}\xi t^{p}-\beta^{t}\xi\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}\beta^{0}\xi-\beta^{t}\xi\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}0,

where step
①
uses
\beta^{t}=\beta^{0}(1+\xi t^{p})
; step
②
uses
(t+1)^{p}-t^{p}\leq 1
for all
p\in(0,1)
; step
③
uses
\beta^{0}\leq\beta^{t}
and
\xi>0
.

Part (b)
. It holds with
\underline{\rm{\ell}}=\overline{\rm{A}}^{2}
and
\overline{\rm{\ell}}=\overline{\rm{A}}^{2}+L_{f}/\beta^{0}
.

∎

C.3
Proof of Lemma
4.4

Proof.

We define
\overline{\rm{X}}\triangleq\sqrt{r}
,
\overline{\rm{z}}\triangleq\|\mathbf{z}^{0}\|+\tfrac{\sigma C_{h}}{2-\sigma}
,
\overline{\rm{y}}\triangleq\overline{\rm{A}}\sqrt{r}+\tfrac{2\overline{\rm{z}}}{\beta^{0}}
, where
\sigma\in[1,2)
.

We let
\underline{\rm{\Theta}}\triangleq F(\bar{\mathbf{X}})-\mu^{0}C_{h}^{2}-C_{h}(\overline{\rm{A}}\sqrt{r}+\overline{\rm{y}})-\tfrac{\overline{\rm{z}}^{2}}{2\beta^{0}}
, where
\bar{\mathbf{X}}
is the optimal solution of Problem (
1
).

Part (a)
. Given
\mathbf{X}^{t+1}\in\mathcal{M}
, we have:
\|\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\overline{\rm{X}}\triangleq\sqrt{r}
.

Part (b)
. We show that
\|\mathbf{z}^{t}\|\leq\overline{\rm{z}}
. For all
t\geq 0
, we have:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\|(\sigma-1)\mathbf{z}^{t}\|+\|(\sigma-1)\mathbf{z}^{t}+\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle(\sigma-1)\|\mathbf{z}^{t}\|+\|\sigma\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t+1})\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle(\sigma-1)\|\mathbf{z}^{t}\|+\sigma C_{h},

step
①
uses the triangle inequality; step
②
uses
\mathbf{z}^{t+1}+(\sigma-1)\mathbf{z}^{t}\in\sigma\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t+1})
, as shown in Lemma
4.1
(
a
); step
③
uses
C_{h}
-Lipschitz continuity of
h(\mathbf{y})
. Applying Lemma
A.6
with
\mathbf{a}_{t}=\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\|
,
c=\sigma C_{h}
, and
\varrho=\sigma-1\in[0,1)
, we have:

\displaystyle\forall t\geq 0,\,\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\|\leq\|\mathbf{z}^{0}\|+\tfrac{c}{1-\varrho}=\|\mathbf{z}^{0}\|+\tfrac{\sigma C_{h}}{2-\sigma}\triangleq\overline{\rm{z}}.

Part (c)
. We show that
\|\mathbf{y}^{t}\|\leq\overline{\rm{y}}
. For all
t\geq 0
, we have:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\tfrac{\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}}{\sigma\beta^{t}}\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})\|+\tfrac{1}{\beta^{0}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\overline{\rm{A}}\sqrt{r}+\tfrac{1}{\beta^{0}}\cdot 2\overline{\rm{z}}\triangleq\overline{\rm{y}},

where step
①
uses the triangle inequality,
\sigma\geq 1
, and
\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\leq\tfrac{1}{\beta^{0}}
; step
②
uses
\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\overline{\rm{A}}\|\mathbf{X}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\overline{\rm{A}}\sqrt{r}
, and
\|\mathbf{z}^{t}\|\leq\overline{\rm{z}}
.

Part (d)
. We show that
\Theta^{t}\geq\underline{\rm{\Theta}}
. For all
t\geq 1
, we have:

\displaystyle\Theta^{t}\triangleq

\displaystyle~L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t},\mu^{t-1})+\mu^{t-1}C_{h}^{2}+\mathbb{T}^{t}+\mathbb{D}^{t}+\mathbb{P}^{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\geq}

\displaystyle~f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-g(\mathbf{X}^{t})+h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})+\langle\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\rangle+\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-g(\mathbf{X}^{t})+h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})+\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}+\mathbf{z}^{t}/\beta^{t}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}/\beta^{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\geq}

\displaystyle~f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-g(\mathbf{X}^{t})+h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t}))-C_{h}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|-\tfrac{1}{2\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\geq}

\displaystyle~f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-g(\mathbf{X}^{t})+h(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t}))-\mu^{t-1}C_{h}^{2}-C_{h}(\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})\|+\|\mathbf{y}^{t}\|)-\tfrac{1}{2\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\geq}

\displaystyle~F(\bar{\mathbf{X}})-\mu^{0}C_{h}^{2}-C_{h}(\overline{\rm{A}}\sqrt{r}+\overline{\rm{y}})-\tfrac{\overline{\rm{z}}^{2}}{2\beta^{0}}\triangleq\underline{\rm{\Theta}},

where step
①
uses the definition of
L(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z},\beta)
and the positivity of
\{\mu^{t},\mathbb{T}^{t},\mathbb{D}^{t},\mathbb{P}^{t}\}
; step
②
uses the
L_{h}
-Lipschitz continuity of
h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y})
, ensuring
h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})\geq h_{\mu^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y})-C_{h}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}\|
, with the specific choice of
\mathbf{y}=\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})
; step
③
uses
h(\mathbf{y})-h_{\mu}(\mathbf{y})\leq\mu C_{h}^{2}
, which has been shown in Lemma
2.3
; step
④
uses
\mu^{t}\leq\mu^{0}
,
\beta^{t}\geq\beta^{0}
,
\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})\|\leq\overline{\rm{A}}\|\mathbf{X}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\overline{\rm{A}}\sqrt{r}
for all
\mathbf{X}\in\mathcal{M}
;
\|\mathbf{y}^{t}\|\leq\overline{\rm{y}}
, and
\|\mathbf{z}^{t}\|\leq\overline{\rm{z}}
.

∎

C.4
Proof of Lemma
4.5

Proof.

We define
L(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z},\beta)\triangleq f(\mathbf{X})-g(\mathbf{X})+h_{\tau/\beta}(\mathbf{y})+\langle\mathbf{z},\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})-\mathbf{y}\rangle+\frac{\beta}{2}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})-\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}
.

We define
\mu^{t}\triangleq\tau/\beta^{t}
. We define
\mathbb{D}^{t}\triangleq\tfrac{\sigma-1}{2-\sigma}\tfrac{2}{\beta^{t-1}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\|_{2}^{2}
.

Part (a)
. We focus on the sufficient decrease for variable
\{\beta\}
. We have:

\displaystyle~\varepsilon_{\beta}\beta^{t}\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\beta^{t+1})-L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\beta^{t})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle~\varepsilon_{\beta}\beta^{t}(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})\cdot\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}+h_{\mu^{t+1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+\tfrac{\beta^{t+1}-\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\varepsilon_{\beta}(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})+\tau C_{h}^{2}(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})+\tfrac{\beta^{t+1}-\beta^{t}}{2(\sigma\beta^{t})^{2}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\varepsilon_{\beta}(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})+\tau C_{h}^{2}(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})+\tfrac{(1+\xi)\beta^{t}-\beta^{t}}{\sigma^{2}\beta^{t}}\tfrac{1}{2\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~(\varepsilon_{\beta}+\tau C_{h}^{2})(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})+\tfrac{\xi}{\sigma^{2}}\tfrac{1}{2\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2},

(36)

where step
①
uses the definition of
L(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)
; step
②
uses Lemma
2.3
, and the fact that
\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}=\tfrac{1}{\sigma\beta^{t}}(\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t})
; step
③
uses
\beta^{t+1}\leq(1+\xi)\beta^{t}
; step
④
uses
\beta^{t}(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})\leq 1
.

Part (b)
. We focus on the sufficient decrease for variable
\{\mathbf{z}\}
. We have:

\displaystyle~\varepsilon_{z}\beta^{t}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\|_{2}^{2}+L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\beta^{t})-L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle~\varepsilon_{z}\beta^{t}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle~(\tfrac{\varepsilon_{z}}{\sigma^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{\sigma})\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2},

(37)

where step
①
uses the definition of
L(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z},\beta)
; step
②
uses
\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}=\sigma\beta^{t}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1})
.

Part (c)
. We focus on the sufficient decrease for variable
\{\mathbf{y}\}
. We have:

\displaystyle~L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle~h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})+\langle\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t}\rangle+\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle~h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})+\langle\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t}+\beta^{t}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\rangle-\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle~h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t}+\tfrac{1}{\sigma}(\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t})\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle~h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\tfrac{1}{2\mu^{t}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑤}}{=}

\displaystyle~(\tfrac{1}{\tau}-1)\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2},

(38)

where step
①
uses the Pythagoras Relation that
\tfrac{1}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{+}-\mathbf{a}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{1}{2}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{a}\|_{2}^{2}=-\tfrac{1}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{+}-\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}+\langle\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{+},\mathbf{a}-\mathbf{y}^{+}\rangle
for all
\mathbf{y},\mathbf{y}^{+},\mathbf{a}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
; step
②
uses
\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}=\sigma\beta^{t}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1})
; step
③
uses
\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})=\mathbf{z}^{t}+\tfrac{1}{\sigma}(\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t})
, as shown in Lemma
4.1
(
a
); step
④
uses the fact that the function
h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y})
is
(1/\mu^{t})
-weakly convex
w.r.t

\mathbf{y}
; step
⑤
uses
\mu^{t}\beta^{t}=\tau
.

Part (d)
. We focus on the sufficient decrease for variable
\{\mathbf{X}\}
. We have:

\displaystyle L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})=\mathfrak{X}.

(39)

Adding Inequalities (
36
), (
37
), (
38
), and (
39
) together, we have:

\displaystyle~\textstyle\varepsilon_{\beta}\beta^{t}\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+\varepsilon_{y}\beta^{t}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon_{z}\beta^{t}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle~+L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\beta^{t+1})-L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})

\displaystyle\overset{}{\leq}

\displaystyle~(\varepsilon_{\beta}+\tau C_{h}^{2})(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})+(\tfrac{1}{2\tau}-\tfrac{1}{2})\beta^{t}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}+(\tfrac{\xi}{2\sigma^{2}}+\tfrac{\varepsilon_{z}}{\sigma^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{\sigma})\cdot\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~(\varepsilon_{\beta}+\tau C_{h}^{2})(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})+(\tfrac{1}{2\tau}-\tfrac{1}{2})\beta^{t}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}+(\tfrac{1}{2\sigma}+\tfrac{1}{4\sigma}+\tfrac{1}{\sigma})\cdot\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~(\varepsilon_{\beta}+\tau C_{h}^{2})(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})+(\tfrac{1}{2\tau}-\tfrac{1}{2})\beta^{t}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\tfrac{2}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~(\varepsilon_{\beta}+\tau C_{h}^{2})(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})+(\tfrac{1}{2\tau}-\tfrac{1}{2})\beta^{t}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle~+\tfrac{2}{\beta^{t}}\{\tfrac{\sigma-1}{2-\sigma}(\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2})+\dot{\sigma}(\beta^{t})^{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\ddot{\sigma}(\tfrac{\beta^{0}}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{\beta^{0}}{\beta^{t+1}})\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\underbrace{(\varepsilon_{\beta}+\tau C_{h}^{2}+\tfrac{2}{\sigma}\ddot{\sigma})}_{\triangleq c}(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})+\underbrace{\tfrac{\sigma-1}{2-\sigma}(\tfrac{2}{\beta^{t-1}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\|_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{2}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2})}_{=\mathbb{D}^{t}-\mathbb{D}^{t+1}}

\displaystyle~+(\tfrac{1}{2\tau}+2\dot{\sigma}-\tfrac{1}{2})\beta^{t}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2},

where step
①
uses
\varepsilon_{z}=\tfrac{1}{4}
,
\xi\leq 1
,
\sigma\geq 1
; step
②
uses
\sigma\geq 1
; step
③
uses the upper bound for
\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}
as shown in Lemma
4.1
(
b
); step
④
uses
\frac{1}{\beta^{t}}\leq\frac{1}{\beta^{t-1}}
. This further leads to:

\displaystyle~\varepsilon_{\beta}\beta^{t}\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+\varepsilon_{y}\beta^{t}\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}^{2}+\varepsilon_{z}\beta^{t}\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathbb{D}^{t+1}-\mathbb{D}^{t}-\tfrac{c}{\beta^{t}}+\tfrac{c}{\beta^{t+1}}

\displaystyle~+L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\beta^{t+1})-L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})

\displaystyle\overset{}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\beta^{t}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot(\tfrac{1}{2\tau}+2\dot{\sigma}-\tfrac{1}{2})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle~\beta^{t}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot(\tfrac{1}{2\tau}+2\tfrac{2\sigma^{2}}{(2-\sigma)^{2}}\tfrac{1}{\tau^{2}}-\tfrac{1}{2})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\beta^{t}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot(\textstyle\tfrac{2-\sigma}{8}+\tfrac{4\sigma}{16}-\tfrac{1}{2})

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle~-\beta^{t}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\underbrace{(\tfrac{2-\sigma}{8})}_{\triangleq\varepsilon_{y}},

where step
①
uses the definition of
\dot{\sigma}\triangleq\tfrac{2\sigma^{2}}{(2-\sigma)^{2}}\tfrac{1}{\tau^{2}}
, as shown in Lemma
4.1
; step
②
uses
\tau\geq\tfrac{4}{2-\sigma}
.

∎

C.5
Proof of Lemma
4.6

Proof.

We define
\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})\triangleq f(\mathbf{X})+\langle\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\rangle+\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}
.

We let
\mathbf{G}^{t}\in\nabla_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-\partial g(\mathbf{X}^{t})
.

We define
\mathbb{P}^{t}\triangleq\tfrac{1}{2}(\alpha+\theta\alpha)\ell(\beta^{t})\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}
.

We define
\varepsilon_{x}^{\prime}\triangleq(\theta-1-\alpha-\theta\alpha)-(1+\xi)(\alpha+\theta\alpha)>0
, and
\varepsilon_{x}\triangleq\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon_{x}^{\prime}\underline{\rm{\ell}}>0
.

First, using the optimality condition of
\mathbf{X}^{t+1}\in\mathcal{M}
, we have:

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{G}^{t}\rangle+\tfrac{\theta\ell(\beta^{t})}{2}\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\leq\langle\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{G}^{t}\rangle+\tfrac{\theta\ell(\beta^{t})}{2}\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}.

(40)

Second, we have:

\displaystyle~L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\textstyle\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})+g(\mathbf{X}^{t})-g(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\textstyle\tfrac{\ell(\beta^{t})}{2}\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}+\langle\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t},\nabla_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})\rangle+\langle\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\partial g(\mathbf{X}^{t})\rangle,

(41)

where step
①
uses the
\ell(\beta^{t})
-smoothness of
\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})
and convexity of
g(\mathbf{X})
.

Third, we derive:

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t},\nabla_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})\rangle

(42)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\cdot\|\nabla_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\cdot\ell(\beta^{t})\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\alpha\ell(\beta^{t})\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\cdot\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\tfrac{\alpha\ell(\beta^{t})}{2}\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}+\tfrac{\alpha\ell(\beta^{t})}{2}\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}},

where step
①
uses the norm inequality; step
②
uses the
\ell(\beta^{t})
-smoothness of
\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})
; step
③
uses
\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t}=\mathbf{X}^{t}+\alpha(\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1})
; step
④
uses
ab\leq\frac{1}{2}a^{2}+\frac{1}{2}b^{2}
for all
a\in\mathbb{R}
and
b\in\mathbb{R}
.

Summing Inequalities (
40
),(
42
), and (
41
), we obtain:

\displaystyle L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{\ell(\beta^{t})}{2}\{(1+\alpha)\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}}+\alpha\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\theta\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\theta\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{\ell(\beta^{t})}{2}\{(1+\alpha)\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}}+(\alpha+\theta\alpha^{2})\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\theta\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}-\alpha(\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1})\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{\ell(\beta^{t})}{2}\{(1+\alpha)\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}}+(\alpha+\theta\alpha^{2})\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\theta(\alpha-1)\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\theta\alpha(\alpha-1)\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{2}(\alpha+\theta\alpha)\ell(\beta^{t})\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}}_{\triangleq\mathbb{P}^{t}}+\tfrac{\ell(\beta^{t})}{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}}\cdot\{1+\alpha+\theta\alpha-\theta\}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathbb{P}^{t}-\mathbb{P}^{t+1}+\tfrac{1}{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}}\cdot\{\ell(\beta^{t})(1+\alpha+\theta\alpha-\theta)+\ell(\beta^{t+1})(\alpha+\theta\alpha)\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathbb{P}^{t}-\mathbb{P}^{t+1}+\tfrac{1}{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}}\cdot\ell(\beta^{t})\{\underbrace{(1+\alpha+\theta\alpha-\theta)+(1+\xi)(\alpha+\theta\alpha)}_{\triangleq-\varepsilon_{x}^{\prime}}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathbb{P}^{t}-\mathbb{P}^{t+1}-\tfrac{1}{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}}\cdot\varepsilon_{x}^{\prime}\cdot\beta^{t}\underline{\rm{\ell}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑤}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathbb{P}^{t}-\mathbb{P}^{t+1}-\varepsilon_{x}\cdot\beta^{t}\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}},

where step
①
uses
\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t}=\mathbf{X}^{t}+\alpha(\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1})
; step
②
uses Lemma
A.1
with
\mathbf{a}=\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}
, and
\mathbf{b}=\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}
; step
③
uses the fact that
\ell(\beta^{t+1})\leq(1+\xi)\ell(\beta^{t})
, which is implied by
\beta^{t+1}\leq(1+\xi)\beta^{t}
; step
④
uses Lemma
4.3
that
\beta^{t}\underline{\rm{\ell}}\leq\ell(\beta^{t})\leq\beta^{t}\overline{\rm{\ell}}
; step
⑤
uses
\varepsilon_{x}\triangleq\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon_{x}^{\prime}\underline{\rm{\ell}}>0
.

∎

C.6
Proof of Lemma
4.7

Proof.

We define:
\Theta^{t}\triangleq L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})+c/\beta^{t}+\mathbb{P}^{t}+\mathbb{D}^{t}
.

Part (a)
. Using Lemma
4.5
, we have:

\displaystyle L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\beta^{t+1})-L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})

(43)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c/\beta^{t}-c/\beta^{t+1}+\mathbb{D}^{t}-\mathbb{D}^{t+1}-\varepsilon_{y}\beta^{t}\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}^{2}-\varepsilon_{z}\beta^{t}\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}^{2}-\varepsilon_{\beta}\beta^{t}\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathfrak{X}.

Using Lemma
4.6
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}\leq\mathbb{P}^{t}-\mathbb{P}^{t+1}-\varepsilon_{x}\beta^{t}\mathcal{X}_{t+1}^{2}.

Adding these two inequalities together and using the definition of
\Theta^{t}
, we have:

\displaystyle\Theta^{t}-\Theta^{t+1}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{\beta}\beta^{t}\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+\varepsilon_{y}\beta^{t}\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}^{2}+\varepsilon_{x}\beta^{t}\mathcal{X}_{t+1}^{2}+\varepsilon_{z}\beta^{t}\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\min(\varepsilon_{y},\varepsilon_{x},\varepsilon_{z},\varepsilon_{\beta})\cdot\beta^{t}\cdot(\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{X}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}^{2}).

Part (b)
. Telescoping this inequality over
t
from 1 to
T
, we have:

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\beta^{t}(\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{X}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}^{2})

\displaystyle~\leq\textstyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\min(\varepsilon_{y},\varepsilon_{x},\varepsilon_{z})}\cdot\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\Theta^{t}-\Theta^{t+1})

\displaystyle~=\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\min(\varepsilon_{y},\varepsilon_{x},\varepsilon_{z})}\cdot(\Theta^{1}-\Theta^{T+1})

\displaystyle~\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\min(\varepsilon_{y},\varepsilon_{x},\varepsilon_{z})}\cdot(\Theta^{1}-\underline{\rm{\Theta}}),

(44)

where step
①
uses
\Theta^{t}\geq\underline{\rm{\Theta}}
. Furthermore, we have:

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\beta^{t}(\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{X}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}^{2})

(45)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}(\beta^{t})^{2}(\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{X}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}^{2})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\beta^{t})^{2}(\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{X}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}^{2})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\geq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{4T\beta^{T}}(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\beta^{t}[(\mathcal{B}_{t+1}+\mathcal{X}_{t+1}+\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}+\mathcal{Z}_{t+1})])^{2},

where step
①
uses
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbf{x}_{i}^{2}\geq\tfrac{1}{n}(\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\mathbf{x}_{i}|)^{2}
for all
\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
.

Combining Inequalities (
44
) and (
45
), we have:

\textstyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\beta^{t}[(\mathcal{B}_{t+1}+\mathcal{X}_{t+1}+\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}+\mathcal{Z}_{t+1})]\leq\textstyle\{\tfrac{\Theta^{1}-\underline{\rm{\Theta}}}{\min(\varepsilon_{y},\varepsilon_{x},\varepsilon_{z},\varepsilon_{\beta})}\cdot 4T\beta^{T}\}^{1/2}=\mathcal{O}(T^{(1+p)/2}).

∎

C.7
Proof of Theorem
4.8

Proof.

We define
e^{t}\triangleq\mathcal{B}_{t}+\mathcal{Z}_{t}+\mathcal{Y}_{t}+\mathcal{X}_{t}
.

We define
\Crit(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z})\triangleq\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})-\mathbf{y}\|+\|\partial h(\mathbf{y})-\mathbf{z}\|+\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{M}}(\nabla f(\mathbf{X})-\partial g(\mathbf{X})+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathbf{z}))\|_{\mathsf{F}}
.

We define
\dot{\mathbf{G}}\triangleq\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\partial g(\mathbf{X}^{t})+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathbf{z}^{t})
.

We define
\ddot{\mathbf{G}}\triangleq\nabla f(\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t})-\partial g(\mathbf{X}^{t})+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathbf{z}^{t}+\beta^{t}\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t})+\theta\ell(\beta^{t})(\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t})
.

We first derive the following inequalities:

\displaystyle~\|\ddot{\mathbf{G}}-\dot{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle~\|\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\nabla f(\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t})-\beta^{t}\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t})-\theta\ell(\beta^{t})(\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~L_{f}\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\beta^{t}\overline{\rm{A}}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|+\theta\ell(\beta^{t})\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~L_{f}\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\beta^{t}\overline{\rm{A}}\{\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|+\overline{\rm{A}}\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\}

\displaystyle~+\theta\ell(\beta^{t})(\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~(L_{f}+\beta^{t}\overline{\rm{A}}^{2}+\theta\ell(\beta^{t}))\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\beta^{t}\overline{\rm{A}}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|+\theta\ell(\beta^{t})\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑤}}{=}

\displaystyle~\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t-1}e^{t})+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t}e^{t+1}),

(46)

where step
①
uses the definitions of
\{\ddot{\mathbf{G}},\dot{\mathbf{G}}\}
; step
②
uses the triangle inequality; step
③
uses the fact that
f(\mathbf{X})
is
L_{f}
-smooth,
\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t}_{{\sf c}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
,
\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}_{{\sf c}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
, and
\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|\leq\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\overline{\rm{A}}\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
, as shown in Lemma
A.11
.

We derive the following inequalities:

\displaystyle\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}\mathcal{M}}(\dot{\mathbf{G}})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}\mathcal{M}}(\dot{\mathbf{G}})+\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t+1}}\mathcal{M}}(\ddot{\mathbf{G}})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2\|\dot{\mathbf{G}}-\ddot{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+2\sqrt{r}\|\dot{\mathbf{G}}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t-1}e^{t})+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t}e^{t+1})+2\sqrt{r}(C_{f}+C_{g}+\overline{\rm{A}}\overline{\rm{z}})\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t-1}e^{t})+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t}e^{t+1}),

where step
①
uses the optimality of
\mathbf{X}^{t+1}
that:

\displaystyle\mathbf{0}=\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t+1}}\mathcal{M}}(\ddot{\mathbf{G}});

step
②
uses the result of Lemma
A.12
by applying

\displaystyle\mathbf{X}=\mathbf{X}^{t},~\tilde{\mathbf{X}}=\mathbf{X}^{t+1},~\mathbf{P}=\dot{\mathbf{G}},~\text{and}~\tilde{\mathbf{P}}=\ddot{\mathbf{G}};

step
③
uses Inequality (
46
), and the fact that
\|\dot{\mathbf{G}}\|=\|\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\partial g(\mathbf{X}^{t})+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathbf{z}^{t}))\|\leq\|\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\partial g(\mathbf{X}^{t})+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathbf{z}^{t})\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq C_{f}+C_{g}+\overline{\rm{A}}\overline{\rm{z}}
.

Finally, we derive:

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Crit(\mathbf{X}^{t},\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t}\|+\|\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|+\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}\mathcal{M}}(\dot{\mathbf{G}})\|_{\mathsf{F}}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|+\|\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|+\|(1-\tfrac{1}{\sigma})(\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1})\|+\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}\mathcal{M}}(\dot{\mathbf{G}})\|_{\mathsf{F}}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t-1}e^{t})+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t}e^{t+1})\}+\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t}e^{t+1})+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t-1}e^{t})\}+\tfrac{1}{T}\mathcal{O}(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tfrac{1}{t^{p}})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑤}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathcal{O}(T^{(p-1)/2})+\mathcal{O}(T^{1-p-1})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑥}}{=}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/3}),

where step
①
uses the definition of
\Crit(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z})
; step
②
uses
\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t+1})\ni(1-\tfrac{1}{\sigma})(\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t})
, as shown in Lemma
4.1
; step
③
uses
\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\|=\|\sigma\beta^{t-1}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t})\|\leq 2\beta^{t}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|=\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t-1}e^{t})
; step
④
uses Lemma
2.5
(
c
) that
\|\bar{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t}\|\leq\mu^{t}C_{h}=\mathcal{O}(\tfrac{1}{t^{p}})
; step
⑤
uses Lemma
A.5
that
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tfrac{1}{t^{p}}\leq\mathcal{O}(T^{1-p})
, and Lemma
4.7
(
b
) that
\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\beta^{t}e^{t+1}\leq\mathcal{O}(T^{(p-1)/2})
; step
⑥
uses the choice
p=1/3
and Lemma
4.7
(
b
).

∎

C.8
Proof of Lemma
4.10

Proof.

We define
\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})\triangleq f(\mathbf{X})+\langle\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\rangle+\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{2}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}
.

We let
\mathbf{G}^{t}\in\nabla_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-\partial g(\mathbf{X}^{t})
. We define
\eta^{t}\triangleq\tfrac{b^{t}\gamma^{j}}{\beta^{t}}\in(0,\infty)
.

Part (a)
. Initially, we show that
\|\mathbf{G}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
is always bounded for
t
with
\mathbf{X}\in\mathcal{M}
. We have:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{G}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\partial g(\mathbf{X}^{t})+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}[\mathbf{z}^{t}+\beta^{t}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t})]\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\|\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\partial g(\mathbf{X}^{t})+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}[\mathbf{z}^{t}+\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{\sigma\beta^{t-1}}(\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1})]\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\|\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\|\partial g(\mathbf{X}^{t})\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\overline{\rm{A}}\cdot\{\|\mathbf{z}^{t}\|+\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{\sigma\beta^{t-1}}(\|\mathbf{z}^{t}\|+\|\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\|)\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle C_{f}+C_{g}+\overline{\rm{A}}\cdot(\overline{\rm{z}}+2(1+\xi)\overline{\rm{z}})\triangleq\overline{g},

where step
①
uses
\mathbf{z}^{t+1}=\mathbf{z}^{t}+\sigma\beta^{t}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1})
; step
②
uses the triangle inequality; step
③
uses
\|\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq C_{f}
,
\|\nabla g(\mathbf{X}^{t})\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq C_{g}
,
\|\nabla\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\|\nabla\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})\|\leq\overline{\rm{A}}
,
\|\mathbf{z}^{t}\|\leq\overline{\rm{z}}
,
\tfrac{1}{\sigma}\leq 1
,
\beta^{t}\leq\beta^{t-1}(1+\xi)
; step
④
uses
\xi\leq 1
.

We derive the following inequalities:

\displaystyle~L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})=\dot{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\dot{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{X}^{t})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle~\{\mathcal{S}^{t}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-g(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})\}-\{\mathcal{S}^{t}(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-g(\mathbf{X}^{t})\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\tfrac{1}{2}\ell(\beta^{t})\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}+\langle\mathbf{G}^{t},\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle~\tfrac{1}{2}\ell(\beta^{t})\|\Retr_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}(-\eta^{t}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho})-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}+\langle\mathbf{G}^{t},\Retr_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}(-\eta^{t}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho})-\mathbf{X}^{t}+\eta^{t}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\rangle-\eta^{t}\langle\mathbf{G}^{t},\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\tfrac{1}{2}\ell(\beta^{t})\|\Retr_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}(-\eta^{t}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho})-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}+\overline{g}\|\Retr_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}(-\eta^{t}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho})-\mathbf{X}^{t}+\eta^{t}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}-\tfrac{\eta^{t}}{\max(1,2\rho)}\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑤}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\tfrac{1}{2}\ell(\beta^{t})\dot{k}\|\eta^{t}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}\overline{g}\ddot{k}\|\eta^{t}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}-\tfrac{\eta^{t}}{\max(1,2\rho)}\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑥}}{=}

\displaystyle~\eta^{t}\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\cdot\{\tfrac{1}{2}\ell(\beta^{t})\dot{k}\tfrac{b^{t}\gamma^{j}}{\beta^{t}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\overline{g}\ddot{k}\tfrac{b^{t}\gamma^{j}}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\max(1,2\rho)}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑦}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\eta^{t}\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\cdot\{(\tfrac{\overline{b}}{2}\dot{k}\overline{\rm{\ell}}+\tfrac{\overline{b}}{2\beta^{0}}\ddot{k}\overline{g})\gamma^{j}-\tfrac{1}{\max(1,2\rho)}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑧}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\eta^{t}\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\cdot\{-\delta\},

(47)

where step
①
uses the definitions of
L(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z},\beta)
; step
②
uses the fact that the function
g(\mathbf{X})
is convex and the function
\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})
is
\ell(\beta^{t})
-smooth
w.r.t.

\mathbf{X}
; step
③
uses
\mathbf{X}^{t+1}=\Retr_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}(-\eta^{t}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho})
; step
④
uses the Cauchy-Schwarz Inequality,
\|\mathbf{G}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\overline{g}
, and Lemma
2.12
(
a
) that
\langle\mathbf{G}^{t},\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\rangle\geq\tfrac{1}{\max(1,2\rho)}\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}
; step
⑤
uses Lemma
2.10
with
\bm{\Delta}\triangleq-\eta^{t}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}
given that
\mathbf{X}^{t}\in\mathcal{M}
and
\bm{\Delta}\in\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}\mathcal{M}
; step
⑥
uses
\eta^{t}\triangleq\tfrac{b^{t}\gamma^{j}}{\beta^{t}}
; step
⑦
uses
\ell(\beta^{t})\leq\beta^{t}\overline{\rm{\ell}}
,
\beta^{0}\leq\beta^{t}
, and
b^{t}\leq\overline{b}
; step
⑧
uses the fact that
\gamma^{j}
is sufficiently small such that:

\displaystyle\gamma^{j}\leq\frac{2(\tfrac{1}{\max(1,2\rho)}-\delta)}{\overline{\rm{\ell}}\dot{k}\overline{b}+\overline{g}\ddot{k}\overline{b}/\beta^{0}}\triangleq\overline{\gamma}.

(48)

Given Inequality (
47
) coincides with the condition of the line search procedure, we complete the proof.

Part (b)
. We derive the following inequalities:

\displaystyle L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})-L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle-\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}\delta{\eta}^{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle-\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}}\delta{\eta}^{t}\cdot\min(1,2\rho)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}}\cdot\delta b^{t}\gamma^{j-1}\gamma\cdot\min(1,2\rho)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}\|^{2}_{\mathsf{F}}\cdot\underbrace{\delta\underline{b}\overline{\gamma}\gamma\cdot\min(1,2\rho)^{2}}_{\triangleq\varepsilon_{x}},

where step
①
uses Inequality (
47
); step
②
uses Lemma
2.12
(
b
) that
\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\geq\min(1,2\rho)\|\mathbb{G}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
; step
③
uses the definition
{\eta}^{t}\triangleq\tfrac{b^{t}\gamma^{j}}{\beta^{t}}
; step
④
uses
b^{t}\geq\underline{b}
, and the following inequality:

\displaystyle\gamma^{j-1}\geq\overline{\gamma}\geq\gamma^{j},

which can be implied by the stopping criteria of the line search procedure.

∎

C.9
Proof of Lemma
4.12

Proof.

We define:
\Theta^{t}\triangleq L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})+c/\beta^{t}+\mathbb{D}^{t}+0\times\mathbb{P}^{t}
,

Part (a)
. Using Lemma
4.5
, we have:

\displaystyle L(\mathbf{X}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\beta^{t+1})-L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})

(49)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c/\beta^{t}-c/\beta^{t+1}+\mathfrak{X}+\mathbb{D}^{t}-\mathbb{D}^{t+1}-\varepsilon_{y}\beta^{t}\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}^{2}-\varepsilon_{z}\beta^{t}\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}^{2}.

Using Lemma
4.10
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}\leq 0\times\mathbb{P}^{t}-0\times\mathbb{P}^{t+1}-\varepsilon_{x}\beta^{t}\mathcal{X}_{t+1}^{2}.

Adding these two inequalities together and using the definition of
\Theta^{t}
, we have:

\displaystyle\Theta^{t}-\Theta^{t+1}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{\beta}\beta^{t}\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+\varepsilon_{y}\beta^{t}\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}^{2}+\varepsilon_{x}\beta^{t}\mathcal{X}_{t+1}^{2}+\varepsilon_{z}\beta^{t}\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\min(\varepsilon_{y},\varepsilon_{x},\varepsilon_{z},\varepsilon_{\beta})\cdot\beta^{t}\cdot(\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{X}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}^{2}).

Part (b)
. Using the same strategy as in deriving Lemma
4.7
(
b
), we finish the proof.

∎

C.10
Proof of Theorem
4.13

Proof.

We define
e^{t}\triangleq\mathcal{B}_{t}+\mathcal{Z}_{t}+\mathcal{Y}_{t}+\mathcal{X}_{t}
.

We define
\Crit(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z})\triangleq\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})-\mathbf{y}\|+\|\partial h(\mathbf{y})-\mathbf{z}\|+\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{M}}(\nabla f(\mathbf{X})-\partial g(\mathbf{X})+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathbf{z}))\|_{\mathsf{F}}
.

We define
\dot{\mathbf{G}}\triangleq\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\partial g(\mathbf{X}^{t})+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathbf{z}^{t})
, and
\ddot{\mathbf{G}}\triangleq\beta^{t}\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t})
.

We let
\mathbf{G}=\mathbf{G}^{t}\in\partial_{\mathbf{X}}L(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})
.

First, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}^{t}\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}^{t}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}^{t}[\mathbf{X}^{t}]^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{G}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle(\dot{\mathbf{G}}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}^{t}\dot{\mathbf{G}}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}^{t}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}^{t}[\mathbf{X}^{t}]^{\mathsf{T}}\dot{\mathbf{G}})+(\ddot{\mathbf{G}}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}^{t}\ddot{\mathbf{G}}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}^{t}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}^{t}[\mathbf{X}^{t}]^{\mathsf{T}}\ddot{\mathbf{G}})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}\mathcal{M}}(\dot{\mathbf{G}})+\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}\mathcal{M}}(\ddot{\mathbf{G}})

where step
①
uses the definition
\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\triangleq\mathbf{G}-\rho\mathbf{X}^{t}\mathbf{G}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}^{t}-(1-\rho)\mathbf{X}^{t}[\mathbf{X}^{t}]^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{G}
, as shown in Algorithm
1
; step
②
uses
\mathbf{G}\in\dot{\mathbf{G}}+\ddot{\mathbf{G}}
; step
③
uses the fact that
\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{M}}(\bm{\Delta})=\bm{\Delta}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbf{X}(\bm{\Delta}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}+\mathbf{X}^{\mathsf{T}}\bm{\Delta})
for all
\bm{\Delta}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}
