Absil et al. (2008a)
. This leads to:

\displaystyle\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}\mathcal{M}}(\dot{\mathbf{G}})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}-\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}\mathcal{M}}(\ddot{\mathbf{G}})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}\mathcal{M}}(\ddot{\mathbf{G}})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\|\ddot{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{}{\leq}

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\beta^{t}\overline{\rm{A}}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|

\displaystyle\overset{}{\leq}

\displaystyle\beta^{t}e^{t+1}+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t-1}e^{t}),

where step
①
uses the triangle inequality; step
②
uses Lemma
2.11
that
\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{M}}(\bm{\Delta})\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\|\bm{\Delta}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
for all
\bm{\Delta}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}
.

Finally, we derive:

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Crit(\mathbf{X}^{t},\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t}\|+\|\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t}\|+\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}\mathcal{M}}(\dot{\mathbf{G}})\|_{\mathsf{F}}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|+\|\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|+\|(1-\tfrac{1}{\sigma})(\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1})\|+\|\Proj_{\mathbf{T}_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}\mathcal{M}}(\dot{\mathbf{G}})\|_{\mathsf{F}}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t}e^{t+1})+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t-1}e^{t})\}+\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t}e^{t+1})+\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t-1}e^{t})\}+\tfrac{1}{T}\mathcal{O}(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tfrac{1}{t^{p}})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑤}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathcal{O}(T^{(p-1)/2})+\mathcal{O}(T^{1-p-1})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑥}}{=}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/3}),

where step
①
uses the definition of
\Crit(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z})
; step
②
uses
\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\partial h(\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t+1})\ni(1-\tfrac{1}{\sigma})(\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t})
, as shown in Lemma
4.1
; step
③
uses
\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\|=\|\sigma\beta^{t-1}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t})\|\leq 2\beta^{t}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|=\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t-1}e^{t})
; step
④
uses Lemma
2.5
(
c
) that
\|\bar{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\breve{\mathbf{y}}^{t}\|\leq\mu^{t}C_{h}=\mathcal{O}(\tfrac{1}{t^{p}})
; step
⑤
uses Lemma
A.5
that
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tfrac{1}{t^{p}}\leq\mathcal{O}(T^{1-p})
, and Lemma
4.7
(
b
) that
\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\beta^{t}e^{t+1}\leq\mathcal{O}(T^{(p-1)/2})
; step
⑥
uses the choice
p=1/3
and Lemma
4.7
(
b
).

∎

Appendix D
Proofs for Section
5

D.1
Proof of Lemma
5.4

We begin by presenting the following four useful lemmas.

Lemma D.1
.

For both
OADMM-EP
and
OADMM-RR
, we have:

\displaystyle(\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}},\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}},\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}},\mathbf{d}_{\beta})\in\partial\Theta(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t}),

(50)

where
\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}}\triangleq\mathbb{A}^{t}+\beta^{t}\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t})
,
\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}}\triangleq\nabla h_{[\tau/\beta^{t}]}(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t}+\beta^{t}(\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t}))
,
\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}}\triangleq\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}
,
\mathbf{d}_{\beta}\triangleq\tfrac{1}{2}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\partial_{\beta}(h_{[\tau/\beta^{t}]}(\mathbf{y}^{t}))-\tfrac{c}{(\beta^{t})^{2}}
,
\mathbb{A}^{t}\triangleq\partial\iota_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{X}^{t})+\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathbf{z}^{t})
.

Proof.

We define the Lyapunov function as:
\Theta(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z},\beta)\triangleq L(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{z},\beta)+c/\beta
. Using this definition, we can promptly derive the conclusion of the lemma.

∎

Lemma D.2
.

For
OADMM-EP
, we define
\{\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}},\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}},\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}},\mathbf{d}_{\beta}\}
as in Lemma
D.1
. There exists a constant
K
such that:

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\{\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}}\|+\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}}\|+|\mathbf{d}_{\beta}|\}\leq\textstyle K\{\mathcal{B}_{t}+\mathcal{X}^{t}+\mathcal{Y}^{t}+\mathcal{Z}^{t}\}.

(51)

Here,
\mathcal{B}_{t}\triangleq\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t-1}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}})\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t-1}}}
,
\mathcal{X}_{t}\triangleq\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
,
\mathcal{Y}_{t}\triangleq\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|
, and
\mathcal{Z}_{t}\triangleq\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|
.

Proof.

First, we obtain:

\displaystyle~\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbb{A}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}=\|\partial I_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{X}^{t})+\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathbf{z}^{t})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle~\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t-1})-\theta\ell(\beta^{t-1})(\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1})

\displaystyle~+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1})-\beta^{t-1}\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t-1})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}))\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle~\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t-1})-\theta\ell(\beta^{t-1})(\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle~+\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\sigma\beta^{t-1}\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t})-\beta^{t-1}\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t-1})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}))\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}(L_{f}+\theta\ell(\beta^{t-1}))\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle~+\beta^{t-1}\|(\sigma-1)\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t})+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t-1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}))+\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t})\|

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle~\mathcal{O}(\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}})+\mathcal{O}(\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|)+\mathcal{O}(\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|),

(52)

where step
①
uses the optimality of
\mathbf{X}^{t+1}
for
OADMM-EP
that:

\displaystyle~\textstyle\partial I_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\ni

\displaystyle~\textstyle-\theta\ell(\beta^{t})(\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{X}}\mathcal{S}(\mathbf{X}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~-\theta\ell(\beta^{t})(\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t})-\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}[\mathbf{z}^{t}+\beta^{t}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t})];

(53)

step
②
uses the triangle inequality, the
L_{f}
-Lipschitz continuity of
\nabla f(\mathbf{X})
for all
\mathbf{X}
; the
L_{g}
-Lipschitz continuity of
\nabla g(\mathbf{X})
, and the upper bound of
\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}_{{\sf c}}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|
as shown in Lemma
A.11
(
c
); step
③
uses the upper bound of
\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}_{{\sf c}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
, and
\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}=\sigma\beta^{t-1}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t})
.

Part (a)
. We bound the term
\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
. We have:

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle~\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbb{A}^{t}+\beta^{t}\mathcal{A}^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\textstyle\mathcal{O}(\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}})+\mathcal{O}(\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|)+\mathcal{O}(\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|),

where step
①
uses the definition of
\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}}
in Lemma
D.1
; step
②
uses the triangle inequality,
\beta^{t-1}\leq\beta^{t}
, and
\ell(\beta^{t})\leq\beta^{t}\overline{\rm{\ell}}
; step
③
uses Inequality (
52
).

Part (b)
. We bound the term
\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}}\|
. We have:

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}}\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\nabla h_{\tau/\beta^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t}+\beta^{t}(\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t}))\|

(54)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\nabla h_{\tau/\beta^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla h_{\tau/\beta^{t-1}}(\mathbf{y}^{t})+\beta^{t}(\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t}))\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathcal{O}(\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|)+C_{h}\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}(\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{\beta^{t-1}}-1)

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathcal{O}(\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|)+C_{h}\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t-1}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}}\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t-1}}}\cdot\sqrt{\tfrac{\beta^{t-1}}{\beta^{t-1}}-\tfrac{\beta^{t-1}}{\beta^{t}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\mathcal{O}(\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|)+C_{h}\underbrace{\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t-1}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}}\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t-1}}}}_{\triangleq\mathcal{B}_{t}}\cdot\sqrt{1-0},

where step
①
uses the definition of
\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}}
in Lemma
D.1
; step
②
uses the fact that
\mathbf{z}^{t}-\tfrac{1}{\sigma}(\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t+1})=\nabla h_{\mu^{t}}(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})
, as shown in Lemma
4.1
; step
③
uses
\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}(\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t})=\sigma(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t+1})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1})
, and
\beta^{t-1}=\mathcal{O}(\beta^{t})
; step
④
uses
\mathcal{B}_{t}\triangleq\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t-1}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}})\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t-1}}}
.

Part (d)
. We bound the term
\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}|\mathbf{d}_{\beta}|
. We have:

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}|\mathbf{d}_{\beta}|\overset{\text{①}}{=}

\displaystyle~\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\cdot\{\tfrac{1}{2}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\partial_{\beta}(h_{[\tau/\beta^{t}]}(\mathbf{y}^{t}))-\tfrac{c}{(\beta^{t})^{2}}\}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle~\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\{\mathcal{O}(\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|)+\partial_{[\tau/\beta^{t}]}(h_{[\tau/\beta^{t}]}(\mathbf{y}^{t}))\cdot\tfrac{\tau}{(\beta^{t})^{2}}-\tfrac{c}{(\beta^{t})^{2}}\textstyle\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\textstyle\mathcal{O}(\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|)+(\tau C_{h}^{2}+c)\tfrac{1}{(\beta^{t})^{3}}\textstyle

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\textstyle\mathcal{O}(\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|)+(\tau C_{h}^{2}+c)\tfrac{2}{(\xi\beta^{0})^{2}}\cdot\mathcal{B}^{t},\textstyle

where step
①
uses the definition of
\mathbf{d}_{\beta}
in Lemma
D.1
; step
②
uses Lemma
2.3
that the function
h_{\tau/\beta^{t}}(\mathbf{y})
is
C_{h}^{2}
-Lipschitz continuous
w.r.t.

\mu^{t}\triangleq\tau/\beta^{t}
; step
③
uses Lemma
A.10
.

Part (e)
. We bound the term
\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
. We have:
\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}}\|\leq\tfrac{1}{\beta^{0}}\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|
.

Part (f)
. Combining the upper bounds for the terms
\{\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}}\|_{\mathsf{F}},\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}}\|,\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}|\mathbf{d}_{\beta}|,\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}}\|\}
, we finish the proof of this lemma.
∎

Lemma D.3
.

For
OADMM-RR
, we define
\{\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}},\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}},\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}},\mathbf{d}_{\beta}\}
as in Lemma
D.1
. There exists a constant
K
such that :

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\{\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}}\|+\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}}\|+|\mathbf{d}_{\beta}|\}\leq\textstyle K\{\mathcal{B}_{t}+\mathcal{X}^{t}+\mathcal{Y}^{t}+\mathcal{Z}^{t}\},

Here,
\mathcal{B}_{t}\triangleq\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}}
,
\mathcal{X}_{t}\triangleq\|\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\mathbb{G}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
,
\mathcal{Y}_{t}\triangleq\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|
, and
\mathcal{Z}_{t}\triangleq\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|
.

Proof.

We define
\mathbf{G}^{t}\triangleq\nabla f(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\nabla g(\mathbf{X}^{t})+A^{\mathsf{T}}(\mathbf{z}^{t}+\beta^{t}(\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}))
.

We define
\dot{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{X})\triangleq L(\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t},\beta^{t})
, we have:
\nabla\dot{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{X}^{t})=\mathbf{G}^{t}
.

Part (a)
. We bound the term
\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
. We have:

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\partial I_{\mathcal{M}}(\mathbf{X}^{t})+\nabla\dot{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{X}^{t})\|_{\mathsf{F}}

(55)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\nabla\dot{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{X}^{t})-\mathbf{X}^{t}[\nabla\dot{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{X}^{t})]^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{G}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t}[\mathbf{G}^{t}]^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}=\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\max(1,1/\rho)\cdot\|\mathbb{G}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}=\mathcal{O}(\mathcal{X}_{t}),

where step
①
uses Lemma
2.13
; step
②
uses the definitions of
\{\mathbf{G}^{t},\mathbf{D}_{\rho}^{t}\}
as in Algorithm
1
; step
③
uses
\|\mathbb{G}_{1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\max(1,1/\rho)\|\mathbb{G}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
, as shown in Lemma
2.12
(
b
).

Part (b)
. We bound the terms
\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}}\|
,
\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}|\mathbf{d}_{\beta}|
, and
\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
. Considering that the same strategies for updating
\{\mathbf{y}^{t},\beta^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t}\}
are employed, their bounds in
OADMM-RR
are identical to those in
OADMM-ER
.

Part (c)
. Combining the upper bounds for the terms
\{\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}}\|_{\mathsf{F}},\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}}\|,\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}|\mathbf{d}_{\beta}|,\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}}\|\}
, we finish the proof of this lemma.

∎

Now, we proceed to prove the main result of this lemma.

Lemma D.4
.

(
Subgradient Bounds) For both
OADMM-EP
and
OADMM-RR
, there exists a constant
K>0
such that:

\dist(\mathbf{0},\partial\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t}))\leq\beta^{t}Ke^{t}.

Here,
\dist^{2}(\mathbf{0},\partial\Theta({\mathcal{W}}))\triangleq\dist^{2}(\mathbf{0},\partial_{\beta}\Theta({\mathcal{W}}))+\dist^{2}(\mathbf{0},\partial_{\mathbf{X}}\Theta({\mathcal{W}}))+\dist^{2}(\mathbf{0},\partial_{\mathbf{y}}\Theta({\mathcal{W}}))+\dist^{2}(\mathbf{0},\partial_{\mathbf{z}}\Theta({\mathcal{W}}))
.

Proof.

For both
OADMM-EP
and
OADMM-RR
, we have:

\displaystyle\dist(\mathbf{0},\partial\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t}))=

\displaystyle~\sqrt{\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}}\|^{2}+\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}}\|^{2}+|\mathbf{d}_{\beta}|^{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{X}}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{y}}\|+|\mathbf{d}_{\beta}|+\|\mathbf{d}_{\mathbf{z}}\|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle~K\beta^{t}\{\mathcal{X}^{t}+\mathcal{Y}^{t}+\mathcal{B}^{t}+\mathcal{Z}^{t}\},

where step
①
uses
\sqrt{a+b}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}
for all
a,b\geq 0
; step
②
uses Lemmas
D.2
and
D.3
.

∎

D.2
Proof of Theorem
5.6

Proof.

We define
K^{\prime}\triangleq 4K/\min(\varepsilon_{x},\varepsilon_{y},\varepsilon_{z},\varepsilon_{\beta})
.

Firstly, using Assumption
5.1
, we have:

\displaystyle\varphi^{\prime}(\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{\infty}))\cdot\dist(\mathbf{0},\partial\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t}))\geq 1.

(56)

Secondly, given the desingularization function
\varphi(\cdot)
is concave, for any
a,b\in\mathbb{R}
, we have:
\varphi(b)+(a-b)\varphi^{\prime}(a)\leq\varphi(a)
. Applying the inequality above with
a=\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{\infty})
and
b=\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t+1})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{\infty})
, we have:

\displaystyle\textstyle(\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t+1}))\cdot\varphi^{\prime}(\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{\infty}))

(57)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\textstyle\underbrace{\varphi(\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{\infty}))}_{\triangleq\varphi^{t}}-\underbrace{\varphi(\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t+1})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{\infty}))}_{\triangleq\varphi^{t+1}}.

Third, we define
\mathcal{X}_{t}\triangleq\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{t-1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
, and derive the following inequalities for
OADMM-EP
:

\displaystyle\min(\varepsilon_{z},\varepsilon_{y},\varepsilon_{x},\varepsilon_{\beta})\beta^{t}\{\mathcal{B}^{2}_{t+1}+\mathcal{Z}^{2}_{t+1}+\mathcal{Y}^{2}_{t+1}+\mathcal{X}^{2}_{t+1}\}

(58)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\Theta^{t}-\Theta^{t+1}=\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle(\varphi^{t}-\varphi^{t+1})\cdot\tfrac{1}{\varphi^{\prime}(\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{\infty})))}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle(\varphi^{t}-\varphi^{t+1})\cdot\dist(\mathbf{0},\partial\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t}))

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle(\varphi^{t}-\varphi^{t+1})\cdot K\beta^{t}(\mathcal{B}_{t}+\mathcal{Z}_{t}+\mathcal{Y}_{t}+\mathcal{X}_{t}),

where step
①
uses Lemma
4.7
; step
③
uses Inequality (
57
); step
④
uses Inequality (
56
); step
⑤
uses Lemma
5.4
. We further derive the following inequalities:

\displaystyle\textstyle(\mathcal{B}_{t+1}+\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}+\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}+\mathcal{X}_{t+1})^{2}

(59)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle 4\cdot\{\mathcal{B}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}^{2}+\mathcal{X}_{t+1}^{2}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\{4K/\min(\varepsilon_{z},\varepsilon_{y},\varepsilon_{x},\varepsilon_{\beta})\}\cdot(\varphi^{t}-\varphi^{t+1})\cdot(\mathcal{B}_{t}+\mathcal{Z}_{t}+\mathcal{Y}_{t}+\mathcal{X}_{t}),

where step
①
uses the norm inequality that
(a+b+c+d)^{2}\leq 4(a^{2}+b^{2}+c^{2}+d^{2})
for any
a,b,c,d\in\mathbb{R}
; step
②
uses Inequality (
58
).

Fourth, we define
\mathcal{X}_{t}\triangleq\|\tfrac{1}{\beta}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}
, and derive the following inequalities for
OADMM-RR
:

\displaystyle\min(\varepsilon_{z},\varepsilon_{y},\varepsilon_{x},\varepsilon_{\beta})\beta^{t}\{\mathcal{B}^{2}_{t+1}+\mathcal{Z}^{2}_{t+1}+\mathcal{Y}^{2}_{t+1}+\mathcal{X}^{2}_{t+1}\}

(60)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\Theta^{t}-\Theta^{t+1}=\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle(\varphi^{t}-\varphi^{t+1})\cdot\tfrac{1}{\varphi^{\prime}(\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{\infty})))}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle(\varphi^{t}-\varphi^{t+1})\cdot\dist(\mathbf{0},\partial\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t}))

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle(\varphi^{t}-\varphi^{t+1})\cdot K\beta^{t}(\varphi^{t}-\varphi^{t+1})\cdot(\mathcal{B}_{t}+\mathcal{Z}_{t}+\mathcal{Y}_{t}+\mathcal{X}_{t}),

where step
①
uses Lemma
4.12
; step
②
uses Inequality (
57
); step
③
uses Inequality (
56
); step
④
uses Lemma
5.4
. Furthermore, using the similar strategies as in deriving Inequality (
59
), we have:

\displaystyle\textstyle(\mathcal{B}_{t+1}+\mathcal{Z}_{t+1}+\mathcal{Y}_{t+1}+\mathcal{X}_{t+1})^{2}

(61)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\textstyle\{4K/\min(\varepsilon_{z},\varepsilon_{y},\varepsilon_{x},\varepsilon_{\beta})\}\cdot(\varphi^{t}-\varphi^{t+1})\cdot(\mathcal{B}_{t}+\mathcal{Z}_{t}+\mathcal{Y}_{t}+\mathcal{X}_{t}).

Part (a)
. We define
e^{t}\triangleq\mathcal{B}_{t}+\mathcal{X}_{t}+\mathcal{Y}_{t}+\mathcal{Z}_{t}
. Given Inequalities (
59
) and (
61
), we establish the following unified inequality applicable to both
OADMM-EP
and
OADMM-RR
:

\displaystyle(e^{t+1})^{2}\leq e^{t}\cdot\underbrace{\{4K/\min(\varepsilon_{z},\varepsilon_{y},\varepsilon_{x},\varepsilon_{\beta})\}}_{\triangleq K^{\prime}}\cdot(\varphi^{t}-\varphi^{t+1}).

(62)

Part (b)
. Considering Inequality (
62
) and applying Lemma
A.14
with
p^{t}\triangleq K^{\prime}\varphi^{t}
, we have:

\displaystyle\textstyle\forall t,\,\sum_{i=t}^{\infty}e^{i+1}\leq e^{t}+2K^{\prime}\varphi^{t}.

Letting
t=1
, we have:
\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}e^{i+1}\leq\textstyle e^{1}+2K^{\prime}\varphi^{1}
.

∎

D.3
Proof of Lemma
5.8

Proof.

We define
d^{t}\triangleq\sum_{i=t}^{\infty}e^{i+1}
.

Part (a-i)
. For
OADMM-EP
, we have for all
t\geq 1
:
\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{\infty}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}\textstyle\sum_{i=t}^{\infty}\|\mathbf{X}^{i}-\mathbf{X}^{i+1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\textstyle\sum_{i=t}^{\infty}\{\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}-\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t+1}})\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}}+\|\mathbf{X}^{i+1}-\mathbf{X}^{i}\|_{\mathsf{F}}+\|\mathbf{y}^{i+1}-\mathbf{y}^{i}\|+\|\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X}^{i+1})-\mathbf{y}^{i+1}\|\}=\textstyle\sum_{i=t}^{\infty}e^{i+1}\triangleq d^{t}
, where step
①
use the triangle inequality.

Part (a-ii)
. For
OADMM-RR
, we have:
\|\mathbf{X}^{t+1}-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\overset{\text{①}}{=}\|\Retr_{\mathbf{X}^{t}}(-\eta^{t}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho})-\mathbf{X}^{t}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}\dot{k}\|\eta^{t}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{\rho}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}\dot{k}\eta^{t}\max(2\rho,1)\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\overset{\text{④}}{=}\dot{k}\max(2\rho,1)\tfrac{b^{t}\gamma^{j}}{\beta^{t}}\|\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\overset{\text{⑤}}{\leq}\dot{k}\max(2\rho,1)\overline{b}\overline{\gamma}\cdot\|\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}}=\mathcal{O}(\|\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\mathbb{G}^{t}_{1/2}\|_{\mathsf{F}})
, where step
①
uses the update rule of
\mathbf{X}^{t+1}
; step
②
uses Lemma
2.10
; step
③
uses Lemma
2.12
(
c
); step
④
uses the definition of
\eta^{t}\triangleq\tfrac{b^{t}\gamma^{j}}{\beta^{t}}
; step
⑤
uses
b^{t}\leq\overline{b}
, and the fact that
\gamma^{j}\leq\overline{\gamma}
. Furthermore, we derive for all
t\geq 1
:
\|\mathbf{X}^{t}-\mathbf{X}^{\infty}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\textstyle\sum_{i=t}^{\infty}\|\mathbf{X}^{i}-\mathbf{X}^{i+1}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\textstyle\mathcal{O}(\sum_{i=t}^{\infty}\|\tfrac{1}{\beta^{t}}\mathbb{G}_{1/2}^{i}\|_{\mathsf{F}})\overset{}{\leq}\mathcal{O}(\sum_{i=t}^{\infty}e^{i+1})=\mathcal{O}(d^{t})
.

Part (b)
. We define
\varphi^{t}\triangleq\varphi(s^{t})
, where
s^{t}\triangleq\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{\infty})
. We derive:

\displaystyle\textstyle d^{t}

\displaystyle\overset{}{\triangleq}

\displaystyle\textstyle\sum_{i=t}^{\infty}e^{i+1}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle e^{t}+2K^{\prime}\varphi^{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle e^{t}+2K^{\prime}\tilde{c}\cdot[(s^{t})^{\tilde{\sigma}}]^{\frac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{\tilde{\sigma}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle e^{t}+2K^{\prime}\tilde{c}\cdot[\tilde{c}(1-\tilde{\sigma})\cdot\frac{1}{\varphi^{\prime}(s^{t})}]^{\frac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{\tilde{\sigma}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle e^{t}+2K^{\prime}\tilde{c}\cdot[\tilde{c}(1-\tilde{\sigma})\cdot\dist(\mathbf{0},\partial\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t}))]^{\frac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{\tilde{\sigma}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑤}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\textstyle e^{t}+2K^{\prime}\tilde{c}\cdot[\tilde{c}(1-\tilde{\sigma})\cdot\beta^{t}Ke^{t}]^{\frac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{\tilde{\sigma}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{⑥}}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle d^{t-1}-d^{t}+2K^{\prime}\tilde{c}\cdot\{\tilde{c}(1-\tilde{\sigma})\cdot\beta^{t}K(d^{t-1}-d^{t})\}^{\frac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{\tilde{\sigma}}}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle\textstyle d^{t-1}-d^{t}+\underbrace{2K^{\prime}\tilde{c}\cdot[\tilde{c}(1-\tilde{\sigma})K]^{\frac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{\tilde{\sigma}}}}_{\triangleq\nu}\cdot[\beta^{t}(d^{t-1}-d^{t})]^{\frac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{\tilde{\sigma}}},

where step
①
uses
\sum_{i=t}^{\infty}e^{i+1}\leq e^{t}+e^{t-1}+4K^{\prime}\varphi^{t}
, as shown in Theorem
5.6
(
b
); step
②
uses the definitions that
\varphi^{t}\triangleq\varphi(s^{t})
,
s^{t}\triangleq\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t})-\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{\infty})
, and
\varphi(s)=\tilde{c}s^{1-\tilde{\sigma}}
; step
③
uses
\varphi^{\prime}(s)=\tilde{c}(1-\tilde{\sigma})\cdot[s]^{-\tilde{\sigma}}
, leading to
[s^{t}]^{\tilde{\sigma}}=\tilde{c}(1-\tilde{\sigma})\cdot\tfrac{1}{\varphi^{\prime}(s^{t})}
; step
④
uses Assumption
5.1
that
1\leq\dist(\mathbf{0},\partial\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t}))\cdot\varphi^{\prime}(s^{t})
; step
⑤
uses
\dist(\mathbf{0},\partial\Theta({\mathcal{W}}^{t}))\leq\beta^{t}Ke^{t}
for both
OADMM-EP
and
OADMM-RR
, as shown in Lemma
5.4
; step
⑥
uses the fact that
e^{t}=d^{t-1}-d^{t}
.

∎

D.4
Proof of Theorem
5.9

Proof.

Using Lemma
5.8
(
b
), we have:

\displaystyle d^{t}\leq d^{t-1}-d^{t}+\nu\cdot[\beta^{t}(d^{t-1}-d^{t})]^{\frac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{\tilde{\sigma}}}.

(63)

By the definition of
e^{t}\triangleq d^{t-1}-d^{t}\triangleq\mathcal{B}_{t}+\mathcal{X}_{t}+\mathcal{Y}_{t}+\mathcal{Z}_{t}
, there exists a universal constant
\overline{\rm{e}}>0
such that
e^{t}\leq\overline{\rm{e}}
.

We consider three cases for Inequality (
63
).

Part (a)
.
\tilde{\sigma}\in(0,\frac{1}{2}]
. We let
u=\frac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{\tilde{\sigma}}\in[1,\infty)
, and
\zeta=(1-p)u=\tfrac{2}{3}\cdot\frac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{\tilde{\sigma}}\in[\tfrac{2}{3},\infty]
.

From Inequality (
63
), we obtain:

\displaystyle d^{t}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle d^{t-1}-d^{t}+\nu\cdot[\beta^{t}(d^{t-1}-d^{t})]^{u}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle d^{t-1}-d^{t}+\nu^{\prime}\cdot t^{pu}\cdot[d^{t-1}-d^{t}]^{u}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}(t^{-\zeta}),

where step
①
uses
\beta^{t}\leq\nu^{\prime}t^{p}
, where
\nu^{\prime}
is some certain constant; step
②
uses Lemma
A.15
with
c=\nu^{\prime}
and
u>1
.

Part (b)
.
\tilde{\sigma}\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
. We let
u=\frac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{\tilde{\sigma}}\in(0,1)
, and
\zeta=\tfrac{1-p}{1/u-1}=\frac{2}{3}\cdot\frac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{2\tilde{\sigma}-1}\in(0,\infty)
.

We have from Inequality (
63
):

\displaystyle d^{t}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\nu[\beta^{t}]^{u}\cdot(d^{t-1}-d^{t})^{u}+d^{t-1}-d^{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\nu[\beta^{t}]^{u}\cdot(d^{t-1}-d^{t})^{u}+(d^{t-1}-d^{t})^{u}\cdot\overline{\rm{e}}^{1-u}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\nu[\beta^{t}]^{u}\cdot(d^{t-1}-d^{t})^{u}+(d^{t-1}-d^{t})^{u}\cdot\overline{\rm{e}}^{1-u}\cdot(\tfrac{\beta^{t}}{\beta^{0}})^{u}

\displaystyle\overset{}{=}

\displaystyle[\nu+(\beta^{0})^{-u}]\cdot[\beta^{t}]^{u}\cdot(d^{t-1}-d^{t})^{u}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{=}

\displaystyle\nu^{\prime}t^{pu}\cdot(d^{t-1}-d^{t})^{u}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{④}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}(t^{-\zeta}),

where step
①
uses the fact that
\max_{x\in(0,\overline{\rm{e}}]}\frac{x}{x^{u}}\leq\overline{\rm{e}}^{1-u}
if
u\in(0,1)
; step
②
uses
\beta^{0}\leq\beta^{t}
and
u\in(0,1)
; step
③
uses the fact that
\beta^{t}\leq\nu^{\prime\prime}t^{p}
for some certain constant
\nu^{\prime\prime}
, and the choice that
\nu^{\prime}=[\nu+(\beta^{0})^{-u}]\nu^{\prime\prime}
; step
④
uses Lemma
A.16
with
c=\nu^{\prime}
.

Part (c)
.
\tilde{\sigma}\in(\tfrac{1}{4},\tfrac{1}{2}]
. We let
u=\tfrac{1-\tilde{\sigma}}{\tilde{\sigma}}\in[1,3)
, and
\zeta=1-pu=\frac{4\tilde{\sigma}-1}{3\tilde{\sigma}}\in(0,\tfrac{2}{3}]
.

We have from Inequality (
63
):

\displaystyle d^{t}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle d^{t-1}-d^{t}+\nu\cdot[\beta^{t}]^{u}\cdot[d^{t-1}-d^{t}]^{u}\cdot

\displaystyle\overset{\text{①}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(d^{t-1}-d^{t})\cdot(1+\overline{\rm{e}}^{u-1}\nu\cdot[\beta^{t}]^{u})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{②}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(d^{t-1}-d^{t})\cdot\nu^{\prime}t^{pu}

\displaystyle\overset{}{\leq}

\displaystyle d^{t-1}\cdot\tfrac{\nu^{\prime}t^{pu}}{\nu^{\prime}t^{pu}+1}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{③}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}({1}/{\exp(t^{\zeta})}),

where step
①
uses the fact that
\tfrac{x^{u}}{x}\leq\overline{\rm{e}}^{u-1}
for all
u\geq 1
, and
x=d^{t-1}-d^{t}=e^{t}\leq\overline{\rm{e}}
; step
②
uses
(1+\overline{\rm{e}}^{u-1}\nu\cdot[\beta^{t}]^{u})\leq\nu^{\prime}t^{pu}
with
\nu^{\prime}
being some certain constant, which is implied by
\beta^{t}=\mathcal{O}(t^{p})
; step
③
uses Lemma
A.17
with
c=\nu^{\prime}
and
q=pu
.

Part (d)
. Finally, using the fact
\|\mathbf{X}^{T}-\mathbf{X}^{\infty}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\leq\mathcal{O}(d^{T})
as shown in Lemma
D.3
(
b
), we finish the proof of this theorem.

∎

Appendix E
Additional Experiments Details and Results

\blacktriangleright

Datasets
. In our experiments, we utilize several datasets comprising both randomly generated and publicly available real-world data. These datasets are structured as data matrices
\mathbf{D}\in\mathbb{R}^{\dot{m}\times\dot{d}}
. They are denoted as follows: ‘mnist-
\dot{m}
-
\dot{d}
’, ‘TDT2-
\dot{m}
-
d^{\prime}
’, ‘sector-
m^{\prime}
-
d^{\prime}
’, and ‘randn-
\dot{m}
-
\dot{d}
’, where
{\mathrm{randn}(m,n)}
generates a standard Gaussian random matrix of size
m\times n
. The construction of
\mathbf{D}\in\mathbb{R}^{\dot{m}\times\dot{d}}
involves randomly selecting
\dot{m}
examples and
\dot{d}
dimensions from the original real-world dataset, sourced from
http://www.cad.zju.edu.cn/home/dengcai/Data/TextData.html
and
https://www.csie.ntu.edu.tw/~cjlin/libsvm/
. Subsequently, we normalize each column of
\mathbf{D}
to possess a unit norm and center the data by subtracting the mean, denoted as
\mathbf{D}\Leftarrow\mathbf{D}-\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{D}
.

\blacktriangleright

.
We present additional experimental results in Figures
4
,
4
, and
5
. The figures demonstrate that the proposed
OADMM
method generally outperforms the other methods, with
OADMM-EP
surpassing
OADMM-RR
. These results reinforce our previous conclusions.

.

.
